Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) is a cornerstone of enterprise decision-making and is widely used by enterprise users in software such as Power BI and Tableau. In traditional BI workflows, users need to prepare data by (1) identifying relevant tables, (2) performing data transformations, and (3) building join relationships, before they can (4) answer their business questions. These steps can be complex and time-consuming, making BI challenging.
  Given the strong capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in working with data, we study their ability to answer BI questions end-to-end, without requiring users to manually perform the tedious preparation steps. To do this, we harvest a large collection of real-world BI projects from public sources, and manually extract pairs of (questions, ground-truth answers) from real user dashboards. The resulting benchmark, BI-Bench, is the first benchmark to systematically study LLMs' ability on end-to-end BI.
  We find that even frontier LLMs perform poorly on BI-Bench, with less than 50% accuracy. To address their limitations, we design a tool-augmented BI-Agent that decomposes BI workflows into subtasks on structured data, such as search, join, and transform, and orchestrates specialized data management methods across BI stages. Furthermore, we develop a post-training framework that synthesizes training trajectories from real BI projects, enabling BI-Agent to be further post-trained using both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). BI-Agent achieves substantial accuracy gains of up to 40 percentage points with vanilla LLMs, and post-trained BI-Agent yields gains of up to 30 points. Our results highlight the importance of combining tool-augmented reasoning with domain-specific post-training in complex BI workflows, and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [72] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)，一种端到端可训练的稀疏注意力架构，通过从query预测动态阈值实现自适应稀疏，训练时用乘法抑制替代硬删除避免表示崩溃，推理时结合Triton自定义内核实现O(1) KV块筛选，在保持稠密模型质量的同时达到约85%训练稀疏度和2.5倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码时巨大的KV缓存导致严重的内存带宽瓶颈。现有稀疏注意力方法依赖刚性启发式规则选择性加载，会丢弃必要上下文导致质量下降。需要一种能硬件加速且不牺牲稠密模型质量的自适应稀疏机制。

Method: 1. ETA架构：从query表示直接预测动态上下文阈值，为困难检索/推理步骤分配稠密级上下文，剪枝常规token。2. 训练策略：乘法抑制低于阈值的logits向零而非删除，提供平滑均匀注意力底座，消除初始token的局部注意力汇聚。3. 推理优化：Triton自定义解码内核利用缓存的几何概率界在O(1)时间筛选KV块，硬剪枝无信息块并吸收粗粒度GPU块选择误纳的token。4. 离线校准：冻结每头常数阈值消除预测器开销，进一步减少27%注意力计算。

Result: 1.45B参数ETA模型在语言建模、常识推理和长上下文针检索任务上媲美稠密注意力，训练稀疏度约85%，解码活跃密度约38%。自定义内核在512K序列长度下比FlashAttention-2快2.5倍。离线校准额外降低27%注意力计算量。

Conclusion: ETA通过可学习的动态阈值和乘法抑制训练，实现了硬件友好的自适应稀疏注意力，在保持稠密模型质量的同时显著降低内存带宽压力并加速解码，为长上下文推理提供了实用的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens. To learn this policy from scratch without representation collapse, ETA \emph{multiplicatively suppresses} sub-threshold logits toward zero during training rather than deleting them. Training against this smooth uniform attention floor provides a distributed probability reservoir that \textbf{causes localized attention sinks on initial tokens to disappear}. It also enables the model to hard-prune uninformative KV blocks at inference time and absorb incidental tokens co-admitted by coarse GPU block selection. As a result, a 1.45B pretrained ETA model rivals dense attention across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and long-context needle retrieval at $\approx 85\%$ training sparsity and $\approx 38\%$ active decode density. At inference time, we implement a custom decode kernel in Triton that screens KV blocks in $O(1)$ time using cached geometric-probabilistic bounds, delivering up to $2.5\times$ wall-clock decode speedups over FlashAttention-2 on sequences up to 512K tokens. Finally, we introduce an offline calibration algorithm for domain-specific deployments that freezes per-head constant thresholds to eliminate predictor overhead, cutting attention compute by an additional $27\%$.

</details>


### [73] [Bio-MF: Low-Latency and High-Fidelity EEG-to-fNIRS Cross-Modal Generation for Hybrid Motor-Imagery Brain--Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20904)
*Boyuan Zhao,Sifan Zhang,Luping Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Bio-MF，一种无潜在空间的一步 MeanFlow 框架，用于 EEG 条件下的 fNIRS 生成，实现了快速、高保真的跨模态合成，显著提升混合运动意象 BCI 解码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 EEG 到 fNIRS 跨模态生成方法存在生成速度慢、需预训练、难以满足实时 MI-BCI 场景需求的问题；虽然一步生成模型可降低延迟，但往往牺牲生成保真度并引入非生理伪影。因此需要一种既快速又能保留任务相关血流动力学结构的生成方法。

Method: Bio-MF 采用无潜在空间的一步 MeanFlow 框架，执行直接信号空间 x-prediction，将输出转化为 MeanFlow 速度监督，仅需一次网络前向即可完成推理。为在异构传感器布局下保留任务相关血流动力学结构，集成了时空交互 4D 编码、跨模态无分类器引导和噪声水平门控 FFT 正则化。

Result: 在 Dataset 1 上，EEG+合成 fNIRS 相较 EEG-only 将 HbR 和 HbO 的分类准确率分别提升 3.37% 和 4.15%；在 Dataset 2 未见 64 通道 EEG 蒙太奇下仍提升 2.98% 和 2.50%。在 RTX PRO 6000 GPU 上单次 fNIRS 试次生成仅需 7.0 ms，比 1000 步 SCDM 加速 857 倍。

Conclusion: Bio-MF 实现了快速且高质量的 EEG 到 fNIRS 合成，保留了下游混合 MI 解码所需的任务相关血流动力学信息，为实时混合 BCI 系统提供了可行的跨模态生成方案。

Abstract: Hybrid motor-imagery brain-computer interfaces (MI-BCIs) combining EEG and fNIRS can outperform EEG-only systems by exploiting complementary electrophysiological and hemodynamic information. To obtain such hybrid information when paired EEG-fNIRS acquisition is unavailable or inconvenient, recent studies have focused on EEG-to-fNIRS cross-modal generation. However, existing methods still suffer from slow generation and often require pretraining, limiting their use in real-time MI-BCI scenarios. Although one-step generative models offer an attractive route to low-latency synthesis, removing the iterative refinement process can reduce generation fidelity and introduce non-physiological artifacts. To address these problems, this paper proposes Bio-MF, a latent-free one-step MeanFlow framework for EEG-conditioned fNIRS generation. Bio-MF performs direct signal-space x-prediction, converts this signal-space output into MeanFlow velocity supervision, and completes inference with one network evaluation. To preserve task-relevant hemodynamic structure under heterogeneous sensor layouts, Bio-MF integrates Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding, cross-modal classifier-free guidance, and noise-level-gated FFT regularization. On Dataset 1, EEG + synthetic fNIRS improves ACC over EEG-only by 3.37 and 4.15 percentage points for HbR and HbO, respectively. On Dataset 2, the corresponding gains remain 2.98 and 2.50 percentage points under the unseen 64-channel EEG montage. On an RTX PRO 6000 GPU, Bio-MF generates one fNIRS trial in 7.0 ms, corresponding to an 857x speedup over the 1000-step SCDM latency. These results show that Bio-MF enables fast EEG-to-fNIRS synthesis while preserving task-relevant generation quality for downstream hybrid MI decoding. Our code is available at https://github.com/psychosiwa/Bio-MF.

</details>


### [74] [Continuous Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent and Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning from History of Astrophysical Time Series Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20906)
*Debartha Paul,Juncheng Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用神经网络参数化的随机微分方程（SDE）建模类星体光变曲线的历史进展，并提出了两个新方法：基于过去离散迭代状态的连续延迟记忆随机梯度下降（CDM-SGD），以及一种无需求解 HJB 偏微分方程的连续时间策略梯度强化学习框架。实验显示 CDM-SGD 在二维地形上比普通 SGD 具有更宽的探索范围和更精确的收敛行为；新 RL 结构的最优性条件可还原为既有的吉布斯策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类星体的随机亮度变化蕴含超大质量黑洞信息，但从地面巡天光变曲线建模这些变化是统计学难题。现有基于 SDE 的神经网络方法虽有进展，仍需改进优化算法的探索能力与收敛精度，同时强化学习中连续时间探索策略通常依赖求解 HJB 方程，计算代价高，亟待更高效的替代方案。

Method: 1. 提出连续延迟记忆随机梯度下降（CDM-SGD），其更新规则依赖离散迭代过程的历史状态，引入延迟记忆机制增强探索。2. 构建连续时间策略梯度强化学习框架，直接优化探索性策略而无需求解 Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) 偏微分方程。理论分析表明其最优性条件可还原为吉布斯策略。

Result: 在二维测试地形上，通过调节超参数，CDM-SGD 表现出比原版 SGD 更宽广的探索行为和更精确的收敛性能。提出的连续时间策略梯度 RL 结构避免了求解 HJB PDE，且其最优性条件与先前工作的吉布斯策略一致，验证了理论正确性。

Conclusion: 本文在类星体光变建模的 SDE-神经网络方法脉络下，贡献了两个正交创新：带延迟记忆的 SGD 变体提升了优化的探索-收敛权衡，免解 HJB 的连续时间策略梯度为探索性强化学习提供了更实用的理论框架。两项成果均通过实验或理论验证，为随机建模与连续控制提供了新工具。

Abstract: Quasars are luminous objects in the universe that exhibit stochastic brightness variations encoding information about the supermassive black holes powering them, and modeling these variations from ground-based survey data time series, known as light curves, is a statistical challenge. This paper reviews how stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have been adapted with neural network parameterizations to overcome this challenge in history. We create the Continuous-Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent which depend on the past state of the discrete iteration process. We performed the simulation on some 2-dimensional landscape and observed some wider-exploration and more precise convergent behavior compared to Vanilla SGD by adjusting hyperparameters. Besides, we proposed a reinforcement learning structure with continuous time policy gradients for exploratory policies without solving HJB PDE, and we show that its optimality conditions recover the Gibbs policy of previous works.

</details>


### [75] [Do Quantum Models Scale Like LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20912)
*David S. Berman,Ying-Jer Kao,Roger G. Melko,Alexander G. Stapleton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究RydbergGPT在量子临界点附近的神经缩放定律，发现临界点附近模型损失随数据集大小呈幂律关系且双点函数统计特性最接近自然语言，支持多尺度依赖促进稳定缩放的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子多体系统临界现象与Transformer神经缩放定律的内在联系，理解模型-数据对缩放行为的统计物理机制。

Method: 在相互作用的Rydberg原子阵列量子比特投影测量数据上训练自回归Transformer（RydbergGPT）；分析不同激光脱调参数下训练损失随数据集规模的缩放关系；引入熵归一化、有限样本校正的互信息双点函数，对比Rydberg测量数据与自然语言语料库的统计结构。

Result: 临界点附近Transformer损失随数据集大小呈幂律（含损失底部修正），远离临界点时幂律描述质量显著下降；临界点附近的双点函数统计特性最接近自然语言，而远离临界点的构型双点函数衰减更快。

Conclusion: 多尺度关联依赖是稳定神经缩放定律的关键因素；缩放行为应被视为特定模型-数据对的固有属性，而非仅取决于模型架构。

Abstract: In this work, we study the neural scaling laws of RydbergGPT, an autoregressive transformer model trained on qubit projective measurement data gathered from interacting Rydberg atom arrays. The quantum system is known to exhibit a finite-size remnant of a critical point as the laser detuning parameter is varied. We find that near the critical point the transformer loss as a function of training dataset size is well described by a power-law with a loss floor correction. However, away from criticality the quality of the power-law description is substantially reduced. We then compare the statistical structure of both Rydberg measurements and natural-language corpora using an entropy-normalised, finite sample corrected mutual information "two-point" function. We find that near-critical statistics of the two point functions are closest to those observed in natural-language, whilst other qubit configurations far from the critical point have two-point functions that decay more rapidly. This supports the hypothesis that multi-scale dependence contributes to stable neural scaling, and that scaling behaviour should be viewed as a property of the model-data pair.

</details>


### [76] [When AI Reviews Train AI Reviewers: Scientific-Judgment Collapse and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20942)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了递归AI同行评审中模型生成的评审进入训练语料导致的“科学判断崩塌”现象，即评分分布压缩和语义多样性下降，并提出TrustReviewer系统，通过训练时精选语料和测试时配对激活转向两种干预措施来缓解该问题，保持判断多样性并提升推荐一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越多参与科学评审，模型生成的评审进入公共数据和未来训练语料，形成递归循环：后续模型从早期模型的判断中学习。这可能导致评审质量退化，出现评分分布压缩和语义多样性降低的“科学判断崩塌”，威胁AI辅助科学评估的可靠性。

Method: 1. 从Llama 3.1 8B起步，先在ICLR 2018-2023官方评审上微调基础评审模型。2. 在ICLR 2024数据上系统性地以不同比例混合官方评审和模型生成评审训练四个后继模型，观察崩塌现象。3. 提出TrustReviewer：训练时在精选语料上单阶段训练核心评审器，减少低质量和语义退化的监督；测试时采用配对激活转向，无需额外训练或专家标注即可缓解残留的崩塌倾向。

Result: 实验表明引入合成评审会压缩评分分布，降低同篇论文和语料库层面的语义多样性，验证了“科学判断崩塌”。TrustReviewer的训练时干预和测试时激活转向能有效保持判断多样性，改善推荐一致性，为递归评审训练的风险提供了具体表征和实用干预。

Conclusion: 递归AI同行评审训练存在具体的判断崩塌风险，会导致评审同质化和质量下降。通过精选训练语料和激活转向等干预，可以在不增加专家标注成本的情况下缓解该问题，为保持AI辅助科学评估的判断多样性和推荐对齐提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly participate in scientific evaluation, both as automated reviewers and as assistants to human reviewers. As model-generated reviews enter public data and future training corpora, AI peer review can become recursive: later reviewers learn from judgments produced by earlier models. We study one step of this feedback loop in a controlled setting. Starting from Llama 3.1 8B, we first fine-tune a reviewer on official ICLR reviews from 2018--2023 and then train four successor models on ICLR 2024 data with systematically varied mixtures of official and model-generated reviews. Our study shows that introducing synthetic reviews compresses rating distributions and reduces both same-paper and corpus-level semantic diversity. We call this pattern $\textbf{scientific-judgment collapse}$.
  To mitigate this failure mode, we introduce $\textbf{TrustReviewer}$, an open-source LLM-based system for generating peer reviews of AI and machine learning papers. TrustReviewer intervenes at two complementary stages. For training-time prevention, we train the core reviewer in a single stage on a curated corpus designed to reduce low-quality and semantically degenerate supervision. For test-time correction, paired activation steering aims to further mitigate residual tendencies toward collapsed judgments without further training or additional expert annotation. Together, these results characterize a concrete risk of recursive reviewer training and provide practical interventions for preserving judgment diversity and improving recommendation alignment in AI-assisted scientific evaluation.

</details>


### [77] [Efficient Bayes-Adaptive Reinforcement Learning with Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20954)
*Jonathan Hau,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的端到端基于模型的强化学习算法，用于在未知环境中高效合成满足线性时序逻辑（LTL）规范的策略。通过将LTL任务表示为极限确定性Büchi自动机（LDBA）并与环境的Bayes自适应马尔可夫决策过程（BAMDP）同步，利用贝叶斯强化学习实现增强的探索-利用权衡，并提出新颖的Bayes自适应蒙特卡洛规划（BAMCP）算法实现近似Bayes最优策略合成。实验证明该方法在性质满足度和样本效率上优于传统无模型方法，且在谨慎强化学习中有效减少训练过程中的任务违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在处理LTL规范任务时，通常采用无模型方法，样本效率低且难以在未知环境中提供形式化保证。需要一种能够结合形式化验证（LTL）与贝叶斯强化学习的方法，以实现高效的探索-利用权衡，并在未知环境中合成满足时序逻辑规范的最优策略。

Method: 1. 将LTL任务转换为极限确定性Büchi自动机（LDBA）；2. 将环境建模为Bayes自适应马尔可夫决策过程（BAMDP）；3. 将LDBA与BAMDP同步构成产品BAMDP；4. 提出新颖的Bayes自适应蒙特卡洛规划（BAMCP）算法，在同步构造中实现近似Bayes最优策略合成；5. 利用贝叶斯强化学习框架实现增强的探索-利用权衡。

Result: 在有限和无限时域任务实验中，该方法在性质满足度和样本效率方面均优于传统无模型方法。消融实验表明新颖的BAMCP算法相较于经典BAMCP在LTL任务满足度上具有显著优势。在谨慎强化学习应用中，成功减少了策略训练过程中的任务违规次数。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将贝叶斯强化学习与LTL形式化规范相结合，通过LDBA-BAMDP同步构造和新颖的BAMCP算法，实现了在未知环境中高效、样本高效且具有形式化保证的策略合成，为安全关键系统的强化学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present a novel end-to-end model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithm for efficient policy synthesis under given Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) specifications (e.g., safety or reachability) in unknown environments. To do so, a Limit-Deterministic B{ü}chi Automaton (LDBA) representation of the LTL task is synchronised with a Bayes-Adaptive Markov Decision Process (BAMDP) representation of the environment, which allows us to leverage an enhanced exploration-exploitation trade-off that is achieved via Bayesian RL, as opposed to traditional non-Bayesian approaches. We further propose a novel Bayes-Adaptive Monte-Carlo Planning (BAMCP) algorithm to allow for approximate Bayes-optimal strategy synthesis in the synchronised BAMDP construct. A range of finite- and infinite-horizon task experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in terms of both property satisfaction and sample efficiency, when compared to traditional model-free approaches. Additional ablation studies also successfully highlight the value of the novel BAMCP algorithm in comparison to classical BAMCP for LTL task satisfaction. Finally, we also showcase a successful application of our approach for \textit{cautious} RL, namely to reduce the number of task violations incurred during policy training.

</details>


### [78] [From Switching to Dynamic Regret: A Simple Reduction via Unbiased Random Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20968)
*Yibo Wang,Wenhao Yang,Sifan Yang,Yuanyu Wan,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个简单框架，将动态后悔最小化归约为切换后悔最小化，通过构造无偏随机序列和代理损失，利用现成的切换后悔算法获得动态后悔界。对于强凸和exp-concave损失达到最优O~(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})，对于一般凸损失恢复O(√(T(1+P_T)))，均匹配极小极大最优界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳在线学习中，动态后悔是衡量在线学习者对抗时变比较序列表现的关键指标。尽管已有诸多进展，但为强凸和exp-concave损失获得最优界通常需要复杂的分析。作者旨在提供一个统一、简单的框架，避免繁琐的特定问题分析，通过归约到切换后悔这一成熟问题来统一求解不同损失类型的动态后悔最优界。

Method: 核心思想：为任意比较序列构造一个辅助随机序列，使其在每轮无偏、方差可控且切换次数可管理。结合合适的代理损失，将动态后悔分解为针对随机序列的期望切换后悔和受控方差项。利用现成的具有切换后悔保证的算法（如强凸/exp-concave情况的Follow-the-Leading-History或类似算法）即可得到动态后悔界。

Result: 理论上证明：对于强凸和exp-concave损失，动态后悔界为O~(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})；对于一般凸损失，恢复O(√(T(1+P_T)))界。这里T为时间范围，P_T为比较序列的路径长度。所有结果均匹配这三类损失的极小极大最优下界。

Conclusion: 提出的归约框架简单通用，将动态后悔最小化转化为切换后悔最小化，统一地为强凸、exp-concave和一般凸损失给出了最优动态后悔界，展示了框架的多功能性和强大表达力。

Abstract: In non-stationary online learning, dynamic regret has attracted increasing attention as a measure of how well an online learner performs against a time-varying comparator sequence. Despite considerable advances, attaining optimal bounds for strongly convex and exp-concave losses often involves intricate analysis. In this paper, we present a \textit{simple} framework that reduces dynamic regret minimization to switching regret minimization. As a result, we can derive dynamic regret bounds by using off-the-shelf algorithms with switching regret guarantees. The key idea of our reduction is to construct, for \textit{any} comparator sequence, an auxiliary random sequence that is unbiased at each round, with the controlled variance and a manageable number of switches. Combining this construction with suitable surrogate losses, we can decompose dynamic regret into the expected switching regret against the random sequence and its controlled variance. Theoretically, for strongly convex and exp-concave losses, we establish the $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ dynamic regret bounds, where $T$ denotes the time horizon and $P_T$ denotes the path-length of the comparator sequence. Moreover, for general convex losses, the same reduction also recovers the $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ dynamic regret bound. Notably, all our findings match the minimax optimal results for these three types of losses, highlighting the versatility of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [79] [Generative inversion for early ranking of competing geologic interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20978)
*Harun Ur Rashid,Daniel O'Malley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种工作流，通过将竞争性地质解释转化为空间先验，并利用水力水头观测数据对其进行排序，以解决地下决策中数据稀缺导致的解释不确定性问题。该方法结合文生图基础模型、变分自编码器、监督逆向网络和稳态流模拟，量化各解释与观测数据的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险地下决策常在严重数据稀缺条件下进行，专家可能对同一地下系统给出相互竞争的解释，但早期缺乏实用方法判断哪个解释最真实。现有方法依赖主观判断或密集数据，难以在早期勘探阶段应用。

Method: 工作流包含：(1) 为每个地质解释训练文生图基础模型生成1600个地质图像集合；(2) 使用解释特定的变分自编码器(VAE)提供潜在表征；(3) 训练监督逆向网络将水头观测映射至潜在空间；(4) 冻结解码器生成图像并映射为对数导电率场；(5) 稳态流模拟预测水头；(6) 将误差转换为高斯形式的兼容性得分进行排序。

Result: 在Johansen组合成基准测试中，三种一致性递减解释的平均水头RMSE分别为0.197(精确且准确)、0.227(准确)、0.280(不匹配)。应用于WIPP地点Culebra白云岩两个概念模型，修正模型获得0.991兼容性权重，原始模型仅0.009，与独立证据一致。

Conclusion: 该框架有效量化了竞争性地质解释与稀疏水力观测数据的一致性，为早期地下决策提供了客观、数据驱动的解释排序工具，无需依赖密集数据或主观判断，显著降低了勘探风险和成本。

Abstract: High-consequence subsurface decisions are often made under severe data scarcity. Experts may arrive at competing interpretations of the same subsurface system, yet early in a project there is rarely a practical way to determine which one is most realistic. This uncertainty can persist until several wells are drilled, often costing millions of dollars. Existing approaches for evaluating geologic interpretations rely either on subjective judgment or on dense data that are rarely available in early-stage investigations. We present a workflow that addresses this challenge by translating competing geologic interpretations into alternative spatial priors and ranking them according to their consistency with hydraulic-head observations. For each interpretation, a text-to-image foundation model generates an ensemble of 1600 geologic images, and a separately trained variational autoencoder provides an interpretation-specific latent representation. A supervised inverse network maps the head observations into this latent space, and the frozen decoder produces an image that is mapped to a log-conductivity field. Steady-state flow simulation then provides predicted heads, and the resulting mismatch is converted into a Gaussian-form compatibility score. We evaluate the framework using a synthetic benchmark based on the Johansen Formation and three interpretations of decreasing consistency with the reference representation. Across 925 test cases, the mean head RMSE increases from 0.197 for the Precise \& Accurate interpretation to 0.227 for the Accurate interpretation and 0.280 for the Mismatched interpretation. We subsequently apply the workflow to two published conceptual models of the Culebra Dolomite Member at the Waste Isolation Pilot Plant. The revised model receives a compatibility weight of 0.991, compared with 0.009 for the original model, consistent with the independent evidence.

</details>


### [80] [ASGARD: Action-Space Guard for UAV Resilience via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20982)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ASGARD，一种双阶段师生流水线，旨在使基于强化学习的无人机控制系统对动作空间攻击具有鲁棒性。教师阶段利用特权信息训练编码器、策略和监视器；学生阶段通过监督学习使编码器和监视器仅依赖无人机物理状态历史即可在板上运行。实验表明ASGARD能抵御各类动作空间攻击，完成任务，且能泛化到未见攻击和隐蔽攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的无人机控制器易受动作空间攻击，即攻击者在策略生成动作后、执行器执行前篡改动作命令。现有防御多针对策略输入攻击，而针对动作空间攻击的防御需在训练时重训练策略，无法在运行时抵御动作损坏。因此需要一种运行时可部署、无需特权信息的防御机制。

Method: ASGARD采用双阶段师生架构：教师阶段中，编码器融合无人机物理状态与动作攻击相关特权信息，生成动作攻击感知的潜在表示，训练RL控制策略和输出修正动作的监视器；学生阶段通过监督学习，使编码器和监视器仅从无人机物理状态历史学习教师行为，实现仅依赖板载状态的实时推理。

Result: 在针对不同动作指令的攻击场景评估中，ASGARD展现出对动作空间攻击的鲁棒性，能在攻击下完成任务。进一实验表明ASGARD能泛化至未见攻击类型，并对隐蔽攻击保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: ASGARD通过师生知识蒸馏，成功将依赖特权信息的教师模型转化为仅需物理状态历史的学生模型，实现了运行时对动作空间攻击的实时检测与修正，显著提升了基于RL的无人机控制系统的安全性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) controllers have been recently adopted for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV) navigation and control. However, they are susceptible to action-space attacks that overwrite the action commands after the policy generates them and before the actuators execute them. While most existing defenses target attacks on the policy's inputs, those addressing action-space attacks retrain the policy at training time and are not resilient to corrupted actions at runtime. We propose ASGARD, a two-phase teacher-student pipeline for making RL-based UAV control resilient to action-space attacks. In the teacher phase, an encoder combines the UAV's physical state with action-attack-related privileged information to produce an action-attack-aware latent that trains the RL control policy and a monitor that outputs corrected action commands to the actuators. In the student phase, both the encoder and the monitor are trained via supervised learning from their teacher counterparts to run on-board using only the UAV's physical state history. We evaluate ASGARD across attack scenarios targeting different action commands on UAV. We find that ASGARD is resilient to action-space attacks and completes the missions despite the attack. We further find that ASGARD generalizes to unseen attacks and remains resilient against stealthy attacks.

</details>


### [81] [From Stress to Affect: Multimodal Deep Learning for Physiological Emotion Recognition Across Wearable Sensor Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20991)
*Desta Haileselassie Hagos,Saurav Keshari Aryal,Legand L. Burge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了Bi-LSTM、TCN、Transformer三种时序深度学习架构在两个多模态可穿戴数据集（WESAD、EmoWear）上的生理情绪识别性能，研究了不同传感器配置（仅手腕、仅胸部、多模态）、采样频率、集成学习等因素的影响。结果显示：没有统一最优架构，性能取决于数据集特性；多模态配置始终优于单模态；4Hz采样频率提供实用的性能-成本平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常仅评估单一模型、传感配置或数据集，缺乏对这些因素如何影响识别性能的系统理解，限制了可穿戴生理情绪识别在实际应用中的指导意义。

Method: 采用参与者独立的留一被试出交叉验证（LOSO-CV），在WESAD和EmoWear两个多模态可穿戴数据集上评估Bi-LSTM、TCN、Transformer三种时序深度学习架构；对比手腕单模态、胸部单模态、多模态三种传感配置；研究软投票集成、传感器消融、采样频率（4Hz至64Hz）及基于梯度的显著性分析。

Result: Transformer在WESAD多模态配置下达到最高准确率（99.02% ± 0.51%）；LSTM在EmoWear多模态配置下唤醒度（91.80% ± 1.06%）和效价（89.96% ± 0.36%）均表现最佳。多模态配置在两个数据集上均显著优于单模态。采样频率分析表明4Hz提供与高频相当的性能且训练成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: 架构性能相对性取决于数据集特性，不存在统一最优架构；多模态感知始终优于单模态；4Hz采样频率是实用的操作点。这些发现为可穿戴生理情绪识别系统的架构选择、传感模态和采样频率提供了指导。

Abstract: Physiological emotion recognition using wearable sensors has important applications in mental health monitoring, affective computing, and human-computer interaction. However, existing studies typically evaluate a single model, sensing configuration, or dataset, limiting our understanding of how these factors influence recognition performance. We present a comparative study of temporal deep learning architectures for physiological emotion recognition using two multimodal wearable datasets: WESAD and EmoWear. Bidirectional long short-term memory (LSTM), temporal convolutional network (TCN), and Transformer models are evaluated under wrist-only, chest-only, and multimodal sensing configurations using participant-independent leave-one-subject-out cross-validation (LOSO-CV). We also investigate soft-voting ensembles, sensor ablation, sampling frequency, and gradient-based saliency. The Transformer achieved the highest multimodal accuracy on WESAD (99.02% +/- 0.51%), whereas the LSTM achieved the best multimodal accuracy on EmoWear for both arousal (91.80% +/- 1.06%) and valence (89.96% +/- 0.36%). These results show that relative architecture performance depends on dataset characteristics rather than one architecture being uniformly superior. Multimodal sensing consistently outperformed wrist-only and chest-only configurations across both datasets. Sampling-frequency analysis showed that 4 Hz provides a practical operating point, with performance comparable to higher frequencies at substantially lower training cost. These findings provide guidance for selecting architectures, sensing modalities, and sampling frequencies for wearable physiological emotion recognition.

</details>


### [82] [MOSAIC-SR: Transformer-Guided Symbolic Regression for Scientific Equation Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20997)
*Peiyi Zheng,Yanming Kang,Hans De Sterck,Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 MOSAIC-SR，结合预训练 Transformer 与符号搜索：Transformer 生成多个初始草图，在表达式空间的多个有希望区域启动搜索，避免随机初始化；搜索过程中联合恢复结构与常数，采用尺度感知常数优化与局部符号修复。在 SRSD-Feynman 等七个基准上，MOSAIC-SR 象征解率均最高，预测精度稳居前二，且对无关哑变量具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归方法面临结构搜索灵活性与推理效率的矛盾：基于搜索的方法虽能细化表达式结构，但依赖昂贵的组合优化且常随机初始化；预训练神经模型生成极快，但预测常含符号错误。需要一种能利用学习先验聚焦有希望区域、并结合数值优化与符号修复精准恢复方程的方法。

Method: MOSAIC-SR 两阶段流程：(1) 预训练 Transformer 根据观测数据生成多个初始草图，作为搜索的多起点，覆盖表达式空间的多个高概率区域；(2) 从每个草图出发进行局部搜索，联合进行结构调整与常数优化——采用尺度感知的常数优化处理不同量纲参数，并引入局部符号修复机制修正结构错误。多起点并行搜索提高找到全局最优的概率。

Result: 在 SRSD-Feynman（含/不含哑变量）及另外六个基准上，MOSAIC-SR 在所有数据集上均获得最高的符号解率，预测精度稳居前两名。引入无关哑变量时性能优势依然保持，表明学习先验能有效聚焦搜索，数值优化与符号修复对方程恢复至关重要。

Conclusion: 将预训练 Transformer 的生成能力作为搜索的先验初始化，配合尺度感知常数优化与局部符号修复，能显著提升符号回归的符号解率与鲁棒性。该范式表明：学习先验聚焦搜索空间、联合结构与常数优化、引入符号层面的修复机制，是实现高效、准确符号回归的关键。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to recover closed-form equations from observations, providing interpretable models for scientific discovery. Existing approaches struggle to combine flexible structural search with efficient inference. Search-based methods can refine expression structure but often rely on costly combinatorial optimization with random initialization. Pretrained neural models generate formulas almost instantly, but their predictions often contain symbolic errors. We introduce MOSAIC-SR, which uses a pretrained Transformer to propose multiple initial sketches. These sketches initialize searches in several promising regions, avoiding random starts in the vast expression space. Each search jointly recovers structure and constants through scale-aware constant optimization and local symbolic repair. We evaluate MOSAIC-SR on the SRSD-Feynman dataset with and without dummy variables and on six additional benchmarks. MOSAIC-SR obtains the highest symbolic solution rate on every dataset while ranking among the top two methods in predictive accuracy. This advantage persists in the presence of irrelevant dummy inputs. The results show that learned priors can focus search on promising equation structures, and that numerical optimization and symbolic repair are important for recovery.

</details>


### [83] [On the Limits of Maximal Coding Rate Reduction for Out-of-Distribution Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21001)
*Menghui Zhou,Gaoshan Bi,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了最大编码率缩减（MCR²）在分布外（OOD）泛化中存在根本性局限：其目标函数单独使用时可达到全局编码最优却导致完全预测失败；即使引入不变性风险最小化（IRM）或风险外推（REx）等不变性原则，也无法消除该失效。可靠的 OOD 保证需要新的假设或学习原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCR² 作为一种信息论框架，为学习结构化、判别性表示及可解释白盒架构提供了有前景的途径。然而，作者观察到 MCR² 在分布偏移下可能完全失效，因此系统研究其 OOD 泛化极限，揭示其理论局限。

Method: 理论分析与反例构造。首先构造反例证明：仅基于不稳定环境特征的表示可达到 MCR² 全局编码最优，但在相关性反转后预测完全失败，即使存在完美稳定特征。其次，证明即使所有测试输入均可在训练中出现，编码质量任意接近最优时预测错误率仍可任意接近 100%。最后，分析引入 IRM/REx 等不变性原则后，失效表示仍共享相同最优编码算子，说明共享编码最优性不蕴含稳定预测。

Result: 确立两大核心局限：1) MCR² 目标单独使用时，最优编码可完全依赖不稳定环境特征，导致 OOD 预测错误率任意接近 100%。2) 直接结合不变性原则（如 IRM、REx）无法消除该失效，因为跨环境共享的最优编码算子不保证预测关系的稳定性。

Conclusion: MCR² 框架若要获得可靠的 OOD 泛化保证，必须引入额外的新假设或学习原则，以在跨环境中建立稳定的预测关系，仅靠编码率最优化或不变性原则均不足够。

Abstract: Substantial efforts have been devoted to making deep learning objectives, representations, and architectures interpretable, with the goal of improving the safety, robustness, and generalisation of learning systems in diverse real-world applications. The recently proposed maximal coding rate reduction ($\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$) offers a promising information-theoretic framework for learning structured, discriminative representations of class-wise submanifolds and has inspired interpretable white-box architectures. However, we observe that $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ can completely fail under distribution shift, motivating our study of its out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation limits. We establish two limitations of $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ for OOD generalisation. First, the $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ objective alone can admit complete prediction failure: a representation based entirely on unstable environmental features can achieve the global coding optimum yet fail completely after correlation reversal, despite an available perfectly stable feature. This exact-optimum example includes test inputs that cannot occur during training. Even when every possible test input can also occur during training, coding quality can be arbitrarily close to optimal while prediction error is arbitrarily close to 100%. Second, directly incorporating the invariance principle underlying widely successful invariant risk minimisation (IRM) and risk extrapolation (REx) does not eliminate this failure. The failing representation admits the same optimal coding operator across training environments, showing that shared coding optimality does not ensure stable prediction. Reliable OOD guarantees for $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ therefore require additional new assumptions or learning principles that establish stable predictive relationships across environments.

</details>


### [84] [Scaling Discovery through Test-Time Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21032)
*Jongho Park,Vasilis Kontonis,Shivam Garg,Akshay Krishnamurthy,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文表明测试时多智能体通信能显著优于独立并行尝试：在ARC-AGI-3基准上，k个通信智能体团队(team@k)的成功率相当于4k个独立智能体，且优势随规模增长而复合。这种增益泛化到研究型任务：多智能体通信在多米诺填充任务上超越best@k及历史最佳分数，在MNIST分类器压缩任务上超越人类最佳方案（4智能体团队生成1,957字节、99.4%准确率的分类器）。但增益有条件：算力受限或缺乏明确进度度量时，独立智能体可能更优；在充足算力和清晰反馈下，多智能体通信持续产出更强结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学进步依赖协作而非孤立，但现有智能体系统很少体现这一特性。通信智能体是否有帮助仍是开放问题，既往结果好坏参半。作者旨在研究测试时通信能否在具有挑战性的新颖问题求解任务上实质性超越独立并行尝试，并探究其规模效应与迁移性。

Method: 采用无预设角色、通过共享目录通信的多智能体测试时通信范式。首先在ARC-AGI-3基准上系统研究通信规模效应（team@k vs 独立智能体），随后将相同范式迁移至多米诺填充和MNIST分类器压缩两个研究导向任务，对比通信团队、best@k独立集成及人类/单智能体最佳基线。

Result: 1) ARC-AGI-3：team@k成功率匹配4k独立智能体，优势随k增大而复合；单智能体无法解决的任务，团队能可靠攻克。2) 多米诺填充：通信智能体超越best@k并刷新历史最佳分数。3) MNIST分类器压缩：4智能体团队生成1,957字节、99.4%准确率模型，体积小于人类最佳方案及单智能体最佳结果。4) 条件性：算力不足或缺乏明确进度信号时，独立智能体可能更优。

Conclusion: 在算力充足且存在清晰反馈信号的条件下，测试时多智能体通信能通过突破性进展的共享使整体持续超越独立并行基线，且收益随规模复合增长。这证实了协作式智能体在新颖问题求解与研究型任务中的根本优势，但也界定了其适用边界。

Abstract: Science advances not in isolation but through collaboration, yet existing agentic systems capture little of this. Whether communicating agents help remains an open question with mixed prior results. We show that test-time communication can substantially outperform independent parallel attempts on challenging tasks, where sharing a breakthrough can push the whole group forward. We first study the effect of scaling multi-agent test-time communication, where agents have no predefined roles and communicate via a shared directory, on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark requiring novel problem solving. We find that a team of $k$ communicating agents, team@$k$, matches the success rate of $4k$ independent agents, and this advantage grows with $k$, suggesting gains compound with scale. The effect is not merely efficiency: a task that no single agent can solve, a team of agents can solve reliably. Furthermore, these gains transfer to research-oriented tasks, given sufficient compute. On polyomino packing, communicating agents outperform best@$k$ and exceed the prior best-known score. On MNIST classifier compression, communication surpasses the best-known human solution. A team of four agents produced a 1,957-byte classifier submission achieving 99.4% test accuracy, smaller than both the best-known human solution and the best single-agent result. These gains are not unconditional. Independent agents may outperform communication when compute is limited or when a clear measure of progress is absent. However, under sufficient compute and clear feedback, multi-agent communication consistently yields stronger results.

</details>


### [85] [Stiefel-AdamW: Geometry-Aware AdamW for Linear Factorization Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21039)
*Emanuele Zangrando,Marco Sutti,Francesco Tudisco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Stiefel-AdamW，一种针对线性分解块（如 LoRA、低秩压缩层、自注意力的 QK 乘积）的优化器，通过将一个因子约束在 Stiefel 流形（正交矩阵）上，另一个因子保持欧几里得，消除了 GL(R^r) 规范对称性导致的因子发散问题，同时保留 AdamW 的坐标级对角预条件优势，仅增加极小开销，在多个模型微调和预训练任务上稳定提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习中普遍存在线性分解块（W = BA），其分解不唯一性（非紧致 GL(R^r) 规范对称性）会导致训练不稳定、限大学习率。现有方法多用标准欧几里得优化器（如 AdamW），忽略了底层几何结构，缺乏对规范对称性的显式处理。

Method: Stiefel-AdamW 将一个因子矩阵约束在 Stiefel 流形（正交矩阵）上，另一个因子保持欧几里得空间，将规范对称性从非紧致 GL(R^r) 放松为紧致正交对称性，防止因子发散。动量估计在环境欧几里得空间进行，几何信息仅通过切空间投影和流形回缩引入，实现近乎无缝替换 AdamW。

Result: 在 GPT2、ViT、Mistral 7B 的 LoRA 微调及 GPT2 在 OpenWebText 上的全量预训练中，Stiefel-AdamW 始终优于强基线，训练更稳定、收敛更快，且计算开销与 AdamW 基本持平。

Conclusion: Stiefel-AdamW 通过几何感知的约束有效解决了线性分解块的规范对称性问题，结合了黎曼优化的稳定性与 AdamW 的实用预条件能力，为含分解结构的模型提供了一个即插即用、理论有保证且开销极低的优化方案。

Abstract: A pervasive structural pattern in modern deep learning is the linear factorization block: a submodule of the form $W = BA$ in which two parameter matrices are multiplied directly, with no intervening nonlinearity. Such blocks appear in LoRA adapters, low-rank compressed layers, query-key products of self-attention, and share a common pathology: the factorization is non-unique, which can destabilize training and limit usable learning rates. Despite this, factorization blocks are typically optimized with standard Euclidean methods that ignore the underlying geometry. We introduce Stiefel-AdamW, a near drop-in replacement for AdamW for use wherever such blocks appear. By constraining one factor on the Stiefel manifold while leaving the other Euclidean, Stiefel-AdamW relaxes the full $\mathrm{GL}(\mathbb{R}^r)$ gauge symmetry to a compact orthogonal symmetry, ruling out factor blow-up while retaining the coordinate-wise diagonal preconditioning that gives AdamW its practical strength. Moment estimation is performed in the ambient Euclidean space, with geometry entering only through a tangent-space projection and a manifold retraction. The implementation overhead over AdamW is minimal, and we show that the resulting optimizer inherits both the stability benefits of Riemannian methods and standard convergence guarantees. We validate Stiefel-AdamW on LoRA-style fine-tuning of GPT2, ViT, and Mistral 7B and on full pretraining of GPT2 on OpenWebText, showing consistent improvements over strong baselines at essentially no additional cost over AdamW.

</details>


### [86] [A Lightweight Plug-in Gate for Transformer-Based Time-Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21044)
*Hongkai Zhuang,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预编码器协变量准入接口，通过轻量级门控机制在编码器处理前调节协变量表示，作为即插即用模块应用于 TimeXer、iTransformer 和 PatchTST 等 Transformer 预测模型，在零额外调优协议下实现了有竞争力的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Transformer 的预测器通常构建协变量表示并直接传递给编码器，缺乏显式的准入/调节阶段。本文研究预编码器协变量准作为输入侧接口，在编码器处理前即时调节该表示。

Method: 实现了一个轻量级的表示级预编码器门控，为表示单元分配 sigmoid 分数；还研究了一个使用正则化变体，惩罚平均准入率。作为插件模块评估于 TimeXer、iTransformer 和 PatchTST，采用零额外调优协议（每个门控模型继承相应基线配置）。实验包括配对预测比较、门控位置消融、初始化消融、受控协变量准入分析，以及基于方差膨胀因子（VIF）的排列特征重要性（PFI）诊断案例研究。

Result: 在 ETTm1、ETTm2、Traffic、Energy 和 ILI 数据集上，门控模块与相应基线具有竞争力；使用惩罚降低了平均准入分数，同时使预测误差保持接近无惩罚的 TimeXer 设置。

Conclusion: 预编码器协变量准入门控提供了一种有竞争力的即插即用模块，能在无需额外调优的情况下调节协变量使用，且使用正则化变体可有效降低协变量准入程度而不显著损害预测精度。

Abstract: Covariate-rich time-series forecasting requires deciding how external variables enter the target forecasting path. Existing Transformer-based forecasters usually build a covariate representation and pass it to the encoder without an explicit admission stage. This paper studies pre-encoder covariate admission as an input-side interface that regulates that representation immediately before encoder processing. We implement the interface with a lightweight representation-level pre-encoder gate that assigns sigmoid scores to representation units, and we also study a usage-regularized variant that penalizes average admission. The interface is evaluated as a plug-in module for TimeXer, Inverted Transformer (iTransformer), and Patch Time Series Transformer (PatchTST) under a zero-extra-tuning protocol, where each gated model inherits the corresponding baseline configuration. Experiments on the Electricity Transformer Temperature minute-level (ETTm1 and ETTm2) datasets, Traffic, Energy, and influenza-like illness (ILI) include paired forecasting comparisons, gate-placement ablation, initialization ablation, controlled covariate-admission analysis, and a variance inflation factor (VIF)-informed permutation feature importance (PFI) diagnostic case study. In the tested settings, the gate is competitive with the corresponding baselines, and the usage penalty reduces average admission scores while keeping forecasting errors close to the unpenalized TimeXer setting.

</details>


### [87] [FedeRage: Provably Convergent Agnostic Federated Learning under General Client Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21057)
*Herlock Rahimi,Dionysis Kalogerias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析了在客户端参与概率未知、偏态且变化的情况下，联邦平均算法实际求解的优化问题，提出了一种名为FedeRage的风险厌恶联邦平均算法，通过嵌入条件风险价值（CVaR）到局部目标中，实现了分布鲁棒优化，仅需每个客户端一个标量，提升了准确性、公平性和收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端参与概率通常未知且分布偏态，现有基于FedAvg的改进方法通常假设服务器知道参与概率，这在实际部署中很少见。因此需要一种无需先验分布信息、能自适应处理非独立同分布数据和随机客户端参与的算法。

Method: 提出Federated Risk-Averse Averaging (FedeRage)，在分布鲁棒优化框架下将条件风险价值（CVaR）嵌入局部目标函数，隐式地上调高损失和低频参与客户端的权重，仅增加每个客户端一个标量参数，保持对参与分布的不可知性。

Result: 理论上证明FedeRage以O(κ/√T)收敛速度收敛，其中κ是风险厌恶代价的上界；实验在三个异构基准上显示其在准确性、公平性和收敛速度上持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: FedeRage通过风险厌恶机制解决了联邦学习中客户端参与分布未知且偏态的问题，无需先验分布信息即可实现更好的性能和公平性，是一种实用且理论有保障的联邦学习算法。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw data, but its performance degrades under non-IID data and stochastic client participation. Remedies built on classical Federated Averaging (FedAvg) typically presuppose that client participation probabilities are known to the server, which is rarely the case in deployed systems. We first discuss and then characterize the optimization problem that \emph{distributionally agnostic} FedAvg actually solves when participation is entirely unknown, possibly highly skewed, and of variable size across rounds: uniform aggregation is shown to minimize a well-defined stochastic objective, weighted by the participation-induced marginal, at a standard $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ rate for convex and possibly nonsmooth losses. Building on this characterization, we propose \emph{Federated Risk-Averse Averaging} (\textsc{FedeRage}), a risk-averse extension of FedAvg that embeds the \emph{Conditional Value-at-Risk} (CVaR) into the local objective within a natural distributionally robust optimization (DRO) framework. \textsc{FedeRage} implicitly upweights high-loss and infrequently participating clients while adding only a \emph{single scalar per-client}, and admits an $\mathcal{O}(κ/\sqrt{T})$ rate in which the factor $κ$ is the upper bound on the ``price" of risk aversion. In contrast with aggregation-alignment schemes based on optimal transport, which require the availability distribution as an input, \textsc{FedeRage} remains agnostic to it. Several experiments on three heterogeneous benchmarks indicate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods in accuracy, fairness, and convergence speed.

</details>


### [88] [Toward individual-level calibration in affect recognition with perceptual adjustment queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21073)
*Xuanzhou Chen,Sankaraleengam Alagapan,Ashwin Pananjady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于感知调整查询（PAQ）的个体化感知校准框架，通过估计每个参与者的刚可觉差（JND）来规范面部情感识别任务中的感知难度，实验验证了该方法能显著均衡个体感知难度并降低反应时方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部情感感知任务假设相同刺激对所有参与者呈现等同的感知难度，但个体感知敏感度差异系统性地违反了这一假设，需要一种个体化的感知难度校准方法。

Method: 提出基于感知调整查询（PAQ）的框架，直接估计每个参与者在面部情感谱系上的刚可觉差（JND），利用PAQ推断的JND重新表达刺激距离，构建感知空间中难度均衡的任务；在双选强制选择（2AFC）任务中，通过二元元认知难度判断和反应时方差分解两种行为指标进行验证。

Result: PAQ校准显著均衡了个体层面的感知任务难度，优于未校准基线和群体水平Weibull校准；同时降低了平均反应时和被试间反应时方差。

Conclusion: PAQ作为一种原则性且实用的个体化感知校准工具，在面部情感识别任务中有效解决了个体感知敏感度差异导致的难度不均衡问题。

Abstract: Behavioral tasks measuring facial affect perception assume that identical stimuli impose equivalent perceptual difficulty across participants. However, this assumption is systematically violated by individual differences in perceptual sensitivity. Using an affective perception task as our testbed, we propose a framework to normalize for perceptual difficulty that directly estimates each participant's Just Noticeable Difference (JND) along the facial affect spectrum via cognitively lightweight perceptual adjustment queries (PAQs). We use these PAQ-inferred JNDs to re-express stimulus distances, constructing difficulty-equated tasks in perceptual space. We validate the framework in a Two-Alternative Forced-Choice (2AFC) task using two complementary behavioral measures: binary metacognitive difficulty judgments and response time variance decomposition. We find that PAQ calibration significantly equalizes perceived task difficulty at an individual level when compared to both the non-calibrated baseline and population-level Weibull calibration, while also reducing mean response time and between-subject variance in response time. These results establish PAQ as a principled and practical instrument for individualized perceptual calibration in facial affect recognition.

</details>


### [89] [REFINEPPO: Learning Continuous Control Policies by Iterative Action Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21108)
*Sachini Weerasekara,Sagar Kamarthi,Jacqueline Isaacs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代动作细化（IAR）方法，通过学习残差修正序列迭代构建控制动作，并与PPO结合形成REFINEPPO，在14个基准任务中达到或超越标准PPO性能，且在多个任务上收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习策略通常采用单次前馈网络直接映射状态到动作，缺乏重新考虑或渐进改进动作的机制，限制了策略在复杂连续控制问题中的表现。

Method: 引入迭代动作细化（IAR）：从初始提议开始，共享细化网络反复以观测状态和当前动作提议为条件，通过学习残差修正序列迭代优化动作；将该机制集成至近端策略优化（PPO）中，形成REFINEPPO算法。

Result: 在14个基准连续控制任务上，REFINEPPO性能匹配或超过标准PPO，并在多个任务上展现更快收敛速度；消融实验验证了细化深度和更新调度的影响。

Conclusion: 迭代动作构建机制使策略能渐进优化动作而非单次预测，为策略学习带来优势，证明了迭代细化在连续控制中的有效性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has achieved strong performance across a wide range of continuous-control problems. These continuous-control policies, however, are often defined as direct mappings from an observed state to an action or action distribution, requiring a single feed-forward network to construct an optimal control decision in one pass. While effective, this formulation leaves little opportunity for the policy to reconsider or progressively improve an action once an initial prediction has been formed. In this work, we explore an alternative approach: rather than learning only to directly predict an action, can a policy learn to iteratively improve one, and can this iterative process provide advantages during policy learning? We introduce Iterative Action Refinement (IAR), an iterative action-construction method that constructs control actions through a sequence of learned residual corrections. Starting from an initial proposal, a shared refinement network repeatedly conditions on the observed state and the current action proposal, allowing each refinement step to revise the action constructed by preceding steps. The final refined proposal is then used to determine the action executed by the agent. We integrate this iterative action-construction mechanism with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), yielding REFINEPPO. We evaluate REFINEPPO across 14 benchmark control tasks, complemented by controlled ablations of refinement depth and update schedules and analyses aimed at understanding why iterative refinement is effective. Across these environments, REFINEPPO matches or exceeds the performance of standard PPO while demonstrating faster convergence on several tasks.

</details>


### [90] [Talk to Me, Jarvis: An Open-Source Edge-Deployable Voice Assistant Framework for Autonomous Racecars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21109)
*Daniel Henel,Frederik Werner,Alexander Langmann,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Jarvis，一个面向自动驾驶高层行为指令的离线语音助手，通过将语音识别、语音合成与自然语言指令分类集成到轻量级本地框架中，解决了在线大模型的网络依赖与推理延迟问题。核心组件是基于 Mistral 7B 领域微调的文本到命令分类器，实现了 97.63% 意图识别准确率和 1.39 秒平均处理延迟，超越更大的在线模型，并开源实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽提升了语音助手的意图理解与上下文感知能力，但在线托管模型引入网络依赖和可变推理延迟，不适用于对时延敏感的自动驾驶场景，因此需要离线、低延迟、高准确的本地语音助手方案。

Method: 构建 Jarvis 离线语音助手架构，集成语音识别 (ASR)、语音合成 (TTS) 与自然语言指令分类；核心文本到命令分类器采用领域特定微调的 Mistral 7B 模型，实现轻量级本地推理。

Result: 实验评估显示，Jarvis 以 97.63% 意图识别准确率和 1.39 秒平均处理延迟，超越更大规模的在线托管模型，满足快速响应操作需求。

Conclusion: Jarvis 证明了离线本地部署的轻量级大模型微调方案可在自动驾驶语音交互中同时实现高准确率与低延迟，为时延关键型自主系统提供了可行的语音助手范式，并开源代码支持后续研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have improved their effectiveness as back-end components for voice assistants, particularly in intent understanding and context-aware input classification. However, online-hosted models introduce network dependency and variable inference latency, limiting their suitability for time-critical autonomous driving applications. In this work, we address these issues by developing Jarvis, an offline voice assistant for high-level behavioral commands of autonomous vehicles. Its architecture integrates speech recognition and synthesis with natural language command classification into a lightweight, local framework. Jarvis core component is a text-to-command classifier, built using a domain-specific fine-tuning of the Mistral 7B model, demonstrating low-latency inference. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that our solution outperforms larger online-hosted models, achieving 97.63 % intent recognition accuracy with an average processing latency of 1.39 s, making it well-suited for operations requiring quick response times. To support further research and fine-tuning, we provide an open-source implementation.

</details>


### [91] [Signal-Centric Remote Sensing via Alternative Preprocessing and Acoustic Processing for ML-Driven Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21123)
*Logan Luna,Sirio Jansen-Sánchez,Ilteris Demirkiran,Leo Ghelarducci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于CSV格式的声纳数据处理替代方案，替代传统的图像表示方法，显著降低了处理时间并提升了检测精度及信噪比等评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声纳数据处理主要依赖图像表示，需要在自主系统上进行图像预处理，存在计算开销大、实时性差等局限性，亟需更高效的数据处理范式。

Method: 采用逗号分隔值（CSV）格式直接处理声纳原始数据，避免图像化转换步骤，结合机器学习算法进行目标检测，构建轻量级远程感知数据处理流水线。

Result: 实验表明：处理时间减少91.18%，机器学习目标检测准确率显著提升，信噪比（SNR）、峰值信噪比（PSNR）等关键评价指标均有改善。

Conclusion: CSV格式声纳数据处理方法在保持或提升检测性能的同时大幅降低计算复杂度，适用于资源受限的自主系统实时远程感知任务，为声纳数据处理提供了新范式。

Abstract: The dominant method of processing sonar data is using image-based representations, requiring the preprocessing of image data on autonomous systems. We propose an alternative data processing method for remote sensing applications via the use of data in Comma-Seperated Value format. Experimentation on our alternative approach shows a reduction of processing time by 91.18%, an improvement in accurate object detection by Machine Learning, and an increase in SNR (Signal-to-noise ratio), PSNR (Peak signal-to-noise ratio), and other evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [92] [Layerwise Decoupling for Stable Structured Sparsification of Fully Connected Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21126)
*Charles Kulick,Armenak Petrosyan,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦的逐层方法，用于结构化稀疏化预训练神经网络的全连接层，该方法比耦合方法更鲁棒，提供更宽的正则化强度可用范围和更低的灾难性过度剪枝率，同时保持可比的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常对所有层联合施加惩罚，容易导致灾难性过度剪枝等问题。研究旨在开发一种更鲁棒的结构化稀疏化方法，能够逐层处理并剪枝神经元、减小层宽度。

Method: 提取浅层两层子网络，归一化内层权重，对每个块的外层权重矩阵施加结构化群体惩罚，按顺序处理层以剪枝神经元并减小层宽度。证明了在最优性下，约束解耦目标等价于对内外层权重的特定联合惩罚，适用于任何正齐次激活函数，从而获得整洁的投影和近端公式。

Result: 在受控分类和稀疏恢复研究中，该方法提供更宽的正则化强度可用范围和更低的灾难性过度剪枝率，同时保持与联合基线可比的准确性。在高维PINN压力测试和OPT-1.3B的前馈层中验证了其适用范围。

Conclusion: 解耦重构比耦合方法更鲁棒，为预训练神经网络全连接层的结构化稀疏化提供了有效的新范式，具有理论保证和实证验证。

Abstract: We propose a decoupled, layerwise method for structurally sparsifying the fully connected layers of pretrained neural networks. Rather than penalizing all layers jointly, our approach extracts shallow two-layer subnetworks, normalizes the inner weights, and applies a structured group penalty to the outer weight matrix of each block, processing layers sequentially to prune neurons and reduce the width of each layer. We prove that the constrained decoupled objective is equivalent at optimality to a specific joint penalty on the inner and outer weights, for any positively homogeneous activation, and thus admits a clean projected and proximal formulation. Our central finding is that this decoupled reformulation is more robust than coupled methods. In numerical experiments it provides a wider usable range of the regularization strength and a lower rate of catastrophic over-pruning than the tested joint baseline while maintaining comparable accuracy. We establish these properties in controlled classification and sparse-recovery studies, and examine their scope in a high-dimensional PINN stress test and in the feed-forward layers of OPT-1.3B.

</details>


### [93] [EnSol: an environment-aware graph neural network for molecular solubility prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21151)
*Thao Nguyen,Saman Shafaei,Zhengyi Zhang,Huimin Zhao,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EnSol 是一个环境感知的概率框架，用于分子溶解度预测，结合溶质-溶剂图表示、交叉注意力机制、温度特征调制和混合密度网络，实现了对溶解度分布的全量预测，在多个基准测试和实验验证中超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子溶解度对反应可行性、制剂性能、分离效率和溶剂选择至关重要，但实验测量成本高且采样稀疏；现有计算模型常假设固定溶剂、确定性公式或简化溶质-溶剂相互作用，难以捕捉复杂分子相互作用、连续温度效应和实验不确定性。

Method: EnSol 将溶质和溶剂表示为分子图，分别学习表征后通过交叉注意力捕捉溶质-溶剂相互作用；温度通过特征级调制直接融入溶剂环境；混合密度网络输出完整溶解度分布，同时建模温度依赖行为和实验不确定性。

Result: 在独立的 SolProp 和 Leeds 基准数据集上，EnSol 的 Spearman 相关系数分别达 0.876 和 0.601，优于现有最先进模型；实验验证中，化学多样性溶质-溶剂对的 Spearman 相关系数为 0.715，支持可靠的溶剂排序。

Conclusion: EnSol 能够在考虑预测不确定性的前提下，跨多样化化学体系实现可靠的溶解度预测和溶剂选择，为分子开发提供了有力的计算工具。

Abstract: Molecular solubility directly affects key aspects of molecular development such as reaction feasibility, formulation performance, separation efficiency, and solvent selection. However, experimental measurement across solutes, solvents, and temperatures remains costly and sparsely sampled. Existing computational models often rely on fixed-solvent assumptions, deterministic formulations, or simplified representations of solute-solvent interactions, limiting their ability to capture complex molecular interactions, continuous temperature effects, and experimental uncertainty. Here, we introduce EnSol, an environment-aware probabilistic framework for molecular solubility prediction. EnSol represents the solute and solvent as molecular graphs and learns separate representations for each before bringing them together through cross-attention to capture solute-solvent interactions. Temperature is incorporated directly into the solvent environment through feature-wise modulation, and a mixture density network predicts full solubility distributions to capture both temperature-dependent behavior and experimental uncertainty. On the independent SolProp and Leeds benchmark datasets, EnSol achieved Spearman correlations of 0.876 and 0.601, respectively, outperforming state-of-the-art solubility prediction models across both benchmarks. Beyond computational benchmarking, experimental validation across chemically diverse solute-solvent pairs showed that EnSol maintained strong predictive performance and supported reliable solvent ranking, achieving a Spearman correlation of 0.715. These results show that EnSol can support reliable solubility prediction and solvent selection across diverse chemical systems while accounting for predictive uncertainty.

</details>


### [94] [HMB-GAN: Hybrid Multi-Bézier GAN for Vector Shape Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21158)
*Elian Hugh Thiele-Evans,Binh Duong Pham,Hani Omar M Alharbi,Liibaan Aaden,Syed Umer Hasnain Zaidi,Prem Prakash Jayaraman,Muhammad Saeed,Boris Eisenbart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HMB-GAN（混合多贝塞尔GAN），一种用于生成CAD就绪矢量几何的端到端可微分生成框架，通过拼接多段贝塞尔曲线构建闭合形状并强制几何连续性。对比量子增强生成器与经典生成器，量子生成器虽收敛更快、参数更少、点云指标略优，但受限于模拟器开销，突显当前量子硬件限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多在光栅化或单贝塞尔域操作，难以生成结构化、CAD就绪的矢量几何。需探索混合量子-经典生成对抗网络在合成多段贝塞尔表示的闭合形状方面的可行性。

Method: 提出HMB-GAN架构，采用端到端可微分框架，通过拼接多段贝塞尔曲线构建闭合形状，几何连续性由构造强制保证。在该架构中对比量子增强生成器与经典生成器，使用点云分布指标和几何形状统计进行评估。

Result: 量子生成器收敛更快、模型参数减少、点云指标略有提升，但受限于经典模拟量子电路的巨大模拟器开销，评估受硬件约束。

Conclusion: 研究证明混合量子架构建模结构化几何的可行性，同时凸显当代量子硬件的局限性。

Abstract: We explore the use of hybrid quantum-classical generative adversarial networks for synthesising CAD-ready vector geometries. Unlike prior work that operates in rasterised or single-Bézier domains, we introduce HMB-GAN (Hybrid Multi-Bézier GAN), an end-to-end differentiable generative framework that constructs closed shapes through stitched multi-segment Bézier representations with geometric continuity enforced by construction. We compare a quantum-enhanced generator with a classical generator within this architecture and evaluate them across point cloud distribution metrics and geometric shape statistics. Results show that despite faster convergence, a reduction in model parameter count, and slightly improved performance on point cloud metrics, the quantum generator suffers from excessive simulator overhead and thus classically-simulated evaluation suffers from hardware constraints. These results demonstrate the feasibility of modelling structured geometries through hybrid quantum architectures whilst highlighting contemporary hardware limitations.

</details>


### [95] [M2G-LLM: Enhancing Clinical Prediction via Multimodal Graph Reasoning and LLM Context Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21164)
*Inyoung Choi,Sukwon Yun,Jiayi Xin,Jie Peng,Tianlong Chen,Qi Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 M2G-LLM 框架，利用图神经网络（GNN）将多模态临床数据（文本、实验室结果、医学影像）融合并注入大语言模型（LLM）中间层，实现跨模态联合推理，在 MIMIC-IV 和 MIMIC-CXR 数据集上显著提升临床预测任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在处理非结构化临床文本方面表现优异，但难以有效整合结构化实验室数据、医学影像等非文本模态，限制了其在临床决策支持中的广泛应用。需要一种能够融合异构多模态数据、捕捉患者时序就诊关系及跨患者相似性传播的框架，以增强 LLM 的多模态推理能力。

Method: M2G-LLM 采用图神经网络构建患者就诊时序图与跨患者相似性图，对齐并聚合临床笔记、实验室结果、胸部 X 光影像等异构模态，生成富含时序与关系信息的多模态上下文向量，随后将该向量注入 LLM 的中间层，实现文本与非文本模态的联合推理。

Result: 在 MIMIC-IV 与 MIMIC-CXR 数据集上的多项临床预测任务（如死亡率预测、再入院预测、表型分类）中，M2G-LLM 均优于纯文本 LLM、多模态融合基线及单独使用 GNN 的方法，验证了图增强多模态注入策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型的语言理解能力与图神经网络的关系推理能力相结合，通过显式建模时序就诊依赖与患者相似性传播，可显著提升多模态临床数据的联合表示与预测性能，为全面的多模态医疗分析提供了可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Integrating diverse data modalities --- such as clinical notes, laboratory results, and medical imaging --- is essential for advancing clinical decision-making. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable performance in processing unstructured clinical text, their limited capacity to incorporate non-text modalities hinders their broader utility in healthcare applications. Here, we introduce M2G-LLM (Multimodal MedGraph-LLM), a novel framework that enhances LLMs with multimodal integration and alignment via Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). Our approach models temporal relationships between patient visits, propagates information across clinically similar patients, and aligns heterogeneous data sources to construct enriched multimodal context vectors. These vectors are injected into the intermediate layers of the LLM, enabling joint reasoning over textual and non-textual modalities. We evaluate M2G-LLM on the MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-CXR datasets, demonstrating improvements in clinical prediction tasks over strong baseline models. Our results highlight the promise of combining the language understanding of LLMs with the relational reasoning capabilities of GNNs for comprehensive, multimodal healthcare analysis.

</details>


### [96] [TierKV: Long-Context On-Device LLMs via Predictive Multi-Tier KV Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21172)
*Zhihao Shu,Md Musfiqur Rahman Sanim,Jie Hu,Kun Yuan,Minghai Qin,Gagan Agrawal,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 TierKV，一种基于预测性多层级缓存优化（PMCO）的移动端 LLM 推理框架，将 KV 缓存按精确、低秩、闪存卸载三层级分配，实现高达 17.6 倍的预填充吞吐提升和 12.5-34% 的内存缓存减少，仅带来微小精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端 LLM 面临 KV 缓存随序列长度线性增长导致的内存瓶颈。现有的低秩压缩、Token 淘汰、闪存卸载等方法分别存在重构开销、不可逆 Token 丢失或 I/O 停顿等问题，抵消了节省内存的收益。

Method: TierKV 采用预测性多层级缓存优化（PMCO）：在解码开始前，利用预填充隐藏状态预测未来缓存需求，在设备内存和精度预算约束下，联合将 Token 分配到精确、低秩、闪存卸载三个层级。该方法保留完整上下文访问，消除反应式淘汰的循环依赖，并提供闭式求解器在运行时确定层级边界和逐层秩。

Result: 在三款移动端 SoC 上跨八个文本、视觉、音频模型评测：预填充吞吐最高提升 17.6 倍，RAM 常驻 KV 缓存减少 12.5-34%，在相同内存预算下支持显著更长的上下文，仅引入微小精度下降。

Conclusion: TierKV 通过预测性多层级缓存优化有效解决了移动端 LLM 的 KV 缓存内存瓶颈，为移动端大模型部署提供了实用的高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are moving onto mobile devices for increasingly diverse workloads over text, images, video, and audio. These applications often require long contexts, making the Key-Value (KV) cache a dominant memory bottleneck because it grows linearly with sequence length and is accessed at every decoding step. Prior work reduces KV-cache footprint through low-rank compression, token eviction, or flash offloading, but the resulting reconstruction overhead, irreversible token loss, or I/O stalls can offset the benefit of saving memory. We present TierKV, a mobile LLM inference framework built on Predictive Multi-Tier Cache Optimization (PMCO). Before decoding starts, PMCO predicts future cache demand from prefill hidden states and jointly assigns tokens to exact, low-rank, and flash-offloaded tiers under the device memory and accuracy budgets. This formulation retains access to the full context, removes the circular dependency of reactive eviction, and admits a closed-form solver that selects tier boundaries and per-layer ranks at runtime. Across eight text, vision, and audio models on three mobile SoCs, TierKV improves prefill throughput by up to 17.6x over existing mobile LLM frameworks, reduces RAM-resident KV cache by 12.5-34%, thereby enabling substantially longer contexts under the same memory budget, while incurring only minor accuracy degradation.

</details>


### [97] [SWE-Proof: Can Language Models Resolve Real-World Issues with Machine-Checked Proofs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21190)
*George Ma,Benjamin Mikek,Haoyu Li,Ferhat Erata,Yuhao Zhang,Zeren Shui,Behrooz Omidvar Tehrani,Jun Huan,Murali Krishna Ramanathan,Somayeh Sojoudi,Hao Zhou,Anoop Deoras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了Benchproofer流水线，将真实代码任务转化为形式化验证任务，构建了SWE-Proof基准（500个形式化验证的真实issue）。实验显示：测试通过的补丁中25%-50%存在反例，形式化规范能将修复率从85%提升至95%，但模型自动编写规范效果不佳（仅62%通过审计），主要失败原因是规范不忠实（仅约束部分行为），规范质量与修复结果强相关，忠实规范合成仍是开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准依赖保留测试套件，天然不完整且易受记忆化影响。形式化验证虽能解决这些问题，但现有工作仅覆盖给定规范的独立任务，无法处理涉及大型仓库、意图模糊的自然语言真实issue。需要一种能将真实编码任务转化为形式化验证任务的方法。

Method: 提出Benchproofer流水线：1)为新代码编写规范；2)用公理总结现有被调用函数；3)通过机械验证门和对抗验证门双重确认才接收实例。将其应用于SWE-bench Verified构建SWE-Proof（500个形式化验证的真实issue），并扩展至SWE-bench Pro。

Result: 在两个前沿模型上：验证发现测试遗漏的问题——25%-50%的测试通过补丁存在反例；结构化自然语言规范无法修复此问题，但正确的形式化规范能将Opus 4.8的修复率从85%提升至95%。模型自行编写规范无增益（仅62%通过审计），主要失败是不忠实（仅约束部分行为）。规范质量与结果强相关：未解决实例中89%规范失败，已解决实例中47%规范失败。

Conclusion: 形式化验证能显著提升代码生成正确性，但核心瓶颈在于高质量形式化规范的自动合成。当前模型难以编写忠实规范（完整约束所需行为），规范质量直接决定修复成败。忠实规范合成是一个具体的开放研究问题，解决它对推进可验证的代码生成至关重要。

Abstract: Ensuring the correctness of LLM-generated code is a core challenge for modern software engineering. Benchmarks for agentic code generation check correctness with held-out test suites, which are inherently incomplete and increasingly susceptible to memorization. Formal verification avoids both problems, but existing work covers only standalone tasks whose specifications are given as input, not real issues, which touch large repositories and state intent in vague natural language. We present Benchproofer, a pipeline that turns a coding task with a known correct patch into a formally verified one: it writes a specification for the new code, summarizes the existing functions that code calls with axioms, and admits an instance only after mechanical and adversarial gates agree. Applying it to SWE-bench Verified yields SWE-Proof, 500 real issues whose correctness is formally verified rather than tested, and it extends to SWE-bench Pro. Across two frontier models, verification catches what tests miss: a quarter to a half of test-passing patches admit counterexamples, which a structured natural-language specification does not fix, while a correct formal one lifts resolution from 85% to 95% for Opus 4.8. Writing that specification is the hard part: models that must write their own gain nothing over an unaided baseline, and only 62% of their specifications pass our audit. The usual failure is faithfulness, a specification that constrains part of the required behavior and leaves the rest free. Specification quality still tracks the outcome, failing on 89% of unresolved instances against 47% of resolved ones, making faithful specification synthesis a concrete open problem.

</details>


### [98] [Reliability-Centered Evaluation of Sparse Longitudinal CT Lesion-Size Forecasting with Conformal Interval Calibration and Gompertz-Inspired Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21197)
*Lingfei Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个基于DeepLesion和DLT的五次访问同一病变轨迹基准，评估了稀疏纵向CT随访中病变大小预测的准确性、不确定性可靠性和轨迹一致性，发现额外历史观测在控制预测时间范围后提供有限预测收益，且预测准确性、不确定性可靠性和轨迹一致性不一定同步改善，需联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏纵向CT随访限制了在仅有少量先前观测时的病变大小预测，需要系统评估不同预测方法在稀疏纵向成像中的表现。

Method: 从DeepLesion和DLT构建五次访问同一病变轨迹基准（205条轨迹，129名患者），采用固定访问索引范围设计预测T3到T4的共同对数变化，逐步加入更早观测，评估预测准确性、不确定性可靠性、事后共形区间校准、亚组表现和Gompertz启发的轨迹正则化。

Result: 评估方法显示点预测准确性部分重叠但不确定性行为不同。十个训练种子的平均保留RMSE在m=1,2,3,4分别为0.4726, 0.4305, 0.4499, 0.4513，m=2时最低RMSE；额外历史未改善RMSE。m=4时，队列水平特征GP覆盖率接近95%名义水平，MC Dropout、深度集成和残差尺度区间偏保守。患者水平共形校准通常产生接近名义或保守覆盖，但区间更宽。患者分组开发交叉验证选择lambda*=0作为Gompertz项。全局人口参考常与病变水平变化方向相反，解剖亚组预测难度不同。

Conclusion: 一旦控制预测时间范围，额外历史观测提供有限预测收益；预测准确性、不确定性可靠性和轨迹一致性不一定同步改善，在稀疏纵向成像中应联合评估。

Abstract: Sparse longitudinal CT follow-up limits lesion-size forecasting when only a few prior observations are available. We constructed a five-visit DLT-derived same-lesion trajectory benchmark from DeepLesion and Deep Lesion Tracker (DLT), yielding 205 trajectories from 129 patients. We compared an exploratory conventional sparse-to-final analysis with a primary fixed visit-index horizon design predicting the common log change from T3 to T4 while progressively adding earlier observations, evaluating predictive accuracy, uncertainty reliability, post-hoc conformal interval calibration, subgroup performance, and Gompertz-inspired trajectory regularization. The evaluated methods showed partially overlapping point-prediction accuracy but distinct uncertainty behavior. Mean held-out RMSE across ten training seeds was 0.4726, 0.4305, 0.4499, and 0.4513 for m = 1, 2, 3, 4, indicating the lowest mean RMSE at m = 2; additional history did not improve RMSE. At m = 4, raw Cohort-Level Feature GP coverage was near the 95% nominal level, whereas MC Dropout, Deep Ensemble, and residual-scale intervals were conservative. Patient-level conformal calibration generally produced near-nominal or conservative coverage at the cost of wider intervals. Patient-grouped development cross-validation selected lambda* = 0 for the Gompertz-inspired term. A global population reference frequently opposed lesion-level change directions, and prediction difficulty varied across anatomical subgroups. Overall, additional historical observations provided limited predictive benefit once the prediction horizon was controlled, while predictive accuracy, uncertainty reliability, and trajectory consistency did not necessarily improve together, and should be evaluated jointly in sparse longitudinal imaging.

</details>


### [99] [MIRCID: Inferred Hub-miRNAs Drive Cross-Task Improvements in Drug Mechanistic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21280)
*Xin Cao,Yigang Chen,Jiatong Xu,Ziyue Zhang,Xiang Cheng,Shenyu Wang,Yangyi Zhang,Xiaoxuan Cai,Shidong Cui,Zihao Zhu,Xiang Ji,Hsi-Yuan Huang,Yang-Chi-Dung Lin,Hsien-Da Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MIRCID框架，利用推断的miRNA表达增强药物作用机制建模，HubmiRNet从L1000标志基因推断414个泛癌hub miRNAs，miRNA增强比TF活性提供更一致的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物MoA建模常依赖扰动转录组，但匹配的miRNA测量常不可用，推断调控特征提供可扩展方式复用这些数据。

Method: 提出MIRCID框架，比较基因表达与推断的TF活性和miRNA表达，用于通路分类和基于相似性的MoA检索。开发HubmiRNet从977个L1000标志基因推断414个泛癌hub miRNAs，并扩展到1,298输出变体。

Result: HubmiRNet实现87.72%皮尔逊相关；1,298输出变体在完整miRNA任务上优于SiCmiR（71.21% vs 67.30%）。miRNA增强提供比TF活性更一致的增益。通用嵌入控制显示模型依赖效用，互补性分析识别出保留基因衍生结构的独特、部分线性可恢复表示。说明性救援案例将改进分类与弱转录特征样本中生物学合理的miRNA模式关联。

Conclusion: 推断的HubmiRs作为转录组数据的生物学信息重编码，支持扰动药物建模，但测量扰动miRNA响应的恢复仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Drug mechanism-of-action (MoA) modeling commonly relies on perturbational transcriptomes, but matched microRNA (miRNA) measurements are often unavailable. Inferred regulatory features offer a scalable way to reuse these data. Here, we present MIRCID, a framework comparing gene expression with inferred transcription factor (TF) activity and miRNA expression across pathway classification and similarity-based MoA retrieval. HubmiRNet infers 414 pan-cancer hub miRNAs (HubmiRs) from 977 L1000 landmark genes, achieving a Pearson correlation coefficient of 87.72\%; its 1,298-output variant also outperformed SiCmiR on the full-miRNA task (71.21\% versus 67.30\%). In the evaluated comparisons, miRNA augmentation provided more consistent gains than TF activity. Generic embedding controls showed model-dependent utility, while complementarity analyses identified a distinct, partially linearly recoverable representation that retained gene-derived structure. Illustrative rescue cases linked improved classification to biologically plausible miRNA patterns in samples with weak transcriptional signatures. These findings support inferred HubmiRs as a biologically informed recoding of transcriptomic data for perturbational drug modeling, while leaving recovery of measured perturbational miRNA responses to further validation.

</details>


### [100] [Multi-Subject Pretraining Enables Short-Calibration Personalization for Closed-Corpus Surface EMG Speech Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21288)
*Chenqian Le,Beatrice Fumagalli,Yasamin Esmaeili,Xupeng Chen,Tianyu He,Nikasadat Emami,Adeen Flinker,Yao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在标准化电极排布、封闭语料库下，利用预训练检查点+多受试者预训练+少量目标受试者微调的流程，将 sEMG 无声语音识别的字符错误率从无校准的 49.3% 降至 21.7%（仅需 3 分钟校准数据即可达到 20.5%），证明了短时校准个性化的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决面部表面肌电（sEMG）无声语音接口在跨受试者泛化性差、校准负担重的问题，探索在极少量数据（人均 21.3 分钟）下实现高精度个性化识别的可行性。

Method: 采用留一受试者出交叉验证：从已发布的单受试者检查点初始化，在非测试受试者数据上预训练，再在目标受试者数据上微调；对比了无校准、直接微调检查点、多受试者随机初始化预训练等基线，并分析校准数据量、适配器模块、评估句子泄露的影响。

Result: 主流程达成 21.7% CER / 31.9% WER，显著优于无校准（49.3% CER）和直接微调检查点（68.0% CER）；多受试者预训练参与者从 1 增至 26 时宏平均 CER 从 74.4% 降至 21.7%；仅 3 分钟校准即得 20.5% CER，与完整 ~13 分钟校准无显著差异；受试者专用适配器无增益；若训练数据包含测试句子则 CER/WER 激增至 78.6%/99.9%。

Conclusion: 在标准化电极排布、封闭语料库条件下，基于预训练检查点的短时（约 3 分钟）目标受试者校准即可实现高精度 sEMG 无声语音识别，为实际部署提供了低负担个性化方案。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG)-based silent speech interfaces are limited by cross-user variability and calibration burden. We study a limited-data setting in which each of 27 speech-typical participants contributed less than 0.5 h of data (21.3 min on average) across Aloud and Mimed speech. Within a closed 50-sentence corpus, we used leave-one-subject-out evaluation, initializing from a released single-subject checkpoint, pretraining on non-held-out participants, and fine-tuning on the target participant. This pipeline achieved 21.7% character error rate (CER) and 31.9% word error rate (WER), compared with 49.3% CER without target-subject calibration and 68.0% CER for direct checkpoint fine-tuning. Multi-subject pretraining from random initialization followed by fine-tuning reached 44.9% CER and did not converge under the fixed schedule in 5 of 27 folds, indicating substantial optimization and accuracy benefits from checkpoint initialization. Macro-averaged CER declined from 74.4% with one pretraining participant to 21.7% with 26. Three minutes of target-subject calibration achieved 20.5% CER and 31.7% WER, with no statistically significant difference from the full approximately 13-min pool (21.7% CER and 31.9% WER). A subject-specific adapter provided no detectable benefit. Excluding the five evaluation sentences from all sEMG model-training data increased CER and WER to 78.6% and 99.9%. These results support short-calibration personalization in a standardized-montage, closed-corpus setting.

</details>


### [101] [FairLMs: A Turnkey Library for Fairness in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21296)
*Jiale Zhang,Michael Larionov,Zichong Wang,Zhipeng Yin,Wenbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairLMs 是一个用于语言模型公平性研究的 Python 库，整合了偏见度量、缓解方法和评估证据，通过显式声明模型能力和输入需求，解决了现有工具接口不兼容的问题，支持 33 个指标、14 个缓解组件、14 个诊断工具、多架构适配器和基准加载器，实现了组件兼容性检查、统一协议下的方法比较及工作流扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型公平性研究工具功能互补但接口各异，导致模型接口、证据格式、访问限制和结果类型难以统一，阻碍了适用性检查和方法对比，亟需一个统一框架来连接度量、缓解和评估全流程。

Method: 构建 FairLMs 库，采用显式声明机制描述模型能力与输入要求，在执行前进行兼容性检查，结果携带获取配置。提供 33 个内在/外在偏见指标、覆盖四大干预类别的 14 个缓解组件、14 个数据集与评分工具诊断、适配三种 Transformer 架构及托管完成 API 的适配器，以及基准数据加载器。

Result: FairLMs 实现了跨工具的组件兼容组合、统一协议下的公平性方法对比，并支持向新模型和数据集的工作流扩展。代码已开源至 GitHub。

Conclusion: FairLMs 通过标准化声明与配置追踪，有效解决了语言模型公平性研究工具链碎片化问题，为可复现、可比较、可扩展的公平性评估提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Fairness research on language models involves measuring bias, applying mitigation methods, and examining the evidence on which an evaluation rests. Existing tools offer complementary functionality through different interfaces, so combining them requires reconciling model interfaces, evidence formats, access constraints, and result types before applicability can be checked or methods compared. We introduce \textbf{FairLMs}, a Python library that connects these activities through explicit declarations of model capabilities and input requirements. It provides 33 intrinsic and extrinsic metrics, 14 mitigation components spanning four intervention categories, 14 dataset and scoring-instrument diagnostics, adapters for the three Transformer architectures and supported hosted completion APIs, and benchmark loaders. Declarations are checked before execution and results carry the configuration under which they were obtained, so that compatible components can be combined, methods compared under a common protocol, and workflows extended to new models and datasets. The source code is available at: https://github.com/FairLMs/FairLMs.

</details>


### [102] [Fast And Accurate Text Content File Type Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21306)
*Manu Nandan,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于识别文本内容文件类型（特别是源代码）的神经网络模型，该模型在准确性和速度上均优于现有工具，且模型体积更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织普遍需要基于文件内容识别文件类型的工具，尤其是在网络安全领域，魔数和文件扩展名不可靠。现有基于模型的工具（如Magika）存在计算负载和检测时间过长的问题，而基于文件解析的工具则存在准确性不足的问题。

Method: 提出一种神经网络模型，专门用于识别文本内容文件类型，特别是源代码文件。该模型设计旨在同时提高准确性、推理速度并减小模型体积。

Result: 在开源文件上的实验表明，该模型在文本内容文件类型识别任务上平均准确率更高，推理速度比Magika快约4倍，模型体积缩小28%。

Conclusion: 所提出的神经网络模型在文本文件类型识别任务中实现了准确性、速度和模型大小的三重优势，为网络安全等场景下的文件类型检测提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: A common requirement across organizations is to have a tool that can identify file types based on their contents, particularly in the cybersecurity domain where magic numbers and file extensions can not be trusted. While existing tools work well in practice, there is plenty of room for improvement either in terms of computational load and time for detection in the case of model based tools like Magika or in terms of accuracy of detection in the case of file parsing tools that use programming language constructs. In this study, we propose a neural network model for identification of types of text content files, especially source code, that is more accurate and faster than other available tools. Our experiments on open-source files indicate that it is not only more accurate on average for text-content file-type identification, but also approximately four times faster than Magika, while being 28% smaller in size.

</details>


### [103] [An Introduction to Compression-Based Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21309)
*John Hurwitz,Edward Raff,Charles K. Nicholas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨无损压缩算法与机器学习的相互转换，提出基于压缩的机器学习设计框架，实验表明其在恶意软件检测上显著优于传统基线，设计选择变化可带来高达0.62的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无损压缩与机器学习之间的循环依赖关系，挖掘压缩技术在现代人工智能和机器学习中的未实现潜力。

Method: 调查并形式化利用压缩进行机器学习的各种策略，引入基于压缩的机器学习设计框架，并通过实验验证。

Result: 基于压缩的方法与传统基线具有竞争力，在恶意软件检测任务上表现决定性更强；改变设计选择可带来高达0.62的准确率增益。

Conclusion: 压缩与机器学习的相互转换提供了有效的设计框架，尤其在恶意软件检测等任务中展现出显著优势，值得进一步探索和应用。

Abstract: Any lossless compression algorithm (like gzip) may be converted into a machine learning method, via either Normalized Compression Distance or the Minimum Description Length principle. Any auto-regressive model may be converted into a lossless compression method via entropy coding. This seemingly circular dependence has unrealized potential in modern artificial intelligence and machine learning, and we survey and formalize the various strategies that have been used to leverage compression for machine learning. We introduce and empirically validate a design framework for compression-based ML, finding compression-based methods competitive with conventional baselines and decisively stronger on malware. We find that varying these design choices yields accuracy gains of up to 0.62.

</details>


### [104] [Deep Reinforcement Learning with Buffered Quantile Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21327)
*Mohammad Alipour-vaezi,Sajad Khodadadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Deep-BQRL 是一种无模型分布式强化学习框架，将缓冲分位数学习扩展到神经网络函数近似，实现可解释的风险敏感决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化分位数强化学习虽提供可解释的风险敏感决策，但点分位数目标学习面临两大挑战：分位数对回报分布微小扰动高度敏感，且精确分位数规划需要计算量大的分布式优化。低缓冲分位数通过平均邻近分位数缓解前一问题，但现有方法仍依赖模型和显式回报分布规划，难以扩展至大规模问题。

Method: 提出 Deep-BQRL：直接从采样转移学习条件回报分位数；从学习的分位数函数相关区域构建缓冲动作评分；利用集成分歧指导探索；采用增强输入表示使策略响应轨迹信息，避免显式复现分位数状态递归。

Result: 在资产出售最优停止问题和滑腻 FrozenLake 实验中，Deep-BQRL 与基于模型的 UCB-BQRL、表格 PPO 和 TRPO 对比。资产出售任务中，Deep-BQRL 在报告目标分位数水平上实现比 PPO 和 TRPO 更小的平均累积点分位数策略差距，UCB-BQRL 差距最小；学习的停止决策随目标分位数变化，直观展示风险敏感行为。

Conclusion: Deep-BQRL 成功将缓冲分位数学习推广至神经网络函数近似，提供了一种无模型、可扩展且保持可解释性的风险敏感强化学习方法，在连续决策任务中优于基线算法。

Abstract: Quantile-based reinforcement learning provides an interpretable approach to risk-sensitive decision-making by optimizing a prescribed quantile of the cumulative-return distribution. Despite this appeal, learning under a point quantile objective is challenging: quantiles can change abruptly under small perturbations of the return distribution, and exact quantile-sensitive planning requires computationally demanding distributional optimization. Lower-buffered quantiles alleviate the former difficulty by averaging neighboring quantiles immediately below the target level, providing a smoother surrogate while preserving the underlying point-quantile objective. Existing methods based on this principle, however, remain model-based and rely on explicit return-law planning, limiting their applicability beyond small tabular problems. We develop Deep-BQRL, a model-free distributional reinforcement-learning framework that extends buffered-quantile learning to neural function approximation. The method learns conditional return quantiles directly from sampled transitions, constructs buffered action scores from the relevant region of the learned quantile function, and uses ensemble disagreement to guide exploration. An augmented input representation allows the learned policy to respond to trajectory information without explicitly reproducing the quantile-state recursion required by exact planning. Experiments on an asset-selling optimal-stopping problem and slippery FrozenLake compare Deep-BQRL with model-based UCB-BQRL and tabular PPO and TRPO implementations. In asset selling, Deep-BQRL attains smaller mean cumulative point-quantile policy gaps than PPO and TRPO at the reported target levels, while UCB-BQRL retains the smallest gaps. The learned stopping decisions also vary with the target quantile, providing an interpretable illustration of the method's risk-sensitive behavior.

</details>


### [105] [Routine Blood Tests Outperform CRP for Distinguishing Bacterial From Viral Infection in Children](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21332)
*Mihaela Demireva,Zhecho Mitev,Djuna Chinareva-Klimentova,Svetoslav Ivanov,Georgi Nalbantov,Dimitar Mitev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用血常规(CBC)和C反应蛋白(CRP)数据，通过机器学习模型（XGBoost表现最佳）区分儿童病毒性与细菌性感染，AUC达81.7%，优于仅基于CRP的决策规则，提示抗生素处方决策应综合多因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急性感染性疾病是儿童就医和住院的主要原因，临床难以区分病毒与细菌感染，导致抗生素滥用和耐药性增加，亟需更准确的辅助诊断工具。

Method: 回顾性研究纳入906例2-14岁确诊病毒或细菌感染的儿童（2022-2026年），收集CBC、CRP及年龄作为特征，训练多种监督分类模型，以AUC、敏感性和特异性评估性能。

Result: XGBoost模型综合所有特征表现最佳，校外验证集AUC 81.7%，敏感性70.8%，特异性79.2%；所有模型AUC均优于仅用CRP的决策规则。

Conclusion: 抗生素处方决策应综合CBC等多因素，而非仅依赖CRP；部分有价值指标尚未纳入常规临床实践，未来应推动多参数联合判读。

Abstract: Acute infectious diseases are among the leading causes of medical consultations and hospitalizations in children worldwide. These infections are predominantly caused by viruses or bacteria, yet differentiating between the two remains a common clinical challenge. As a result, pediatricians often default to the safer option of prescribing antibiotics contributing to the growing problem of antimicrobial resistance. The objective is to assess the additional predictive value of CBC towards determining the current infection. This retrospective study used data from 906 pediatric patients aged between 2 and 14 years who were tested positive either for viral or bacterial infection between 2022 and 2026.
